# CLAIMS register — Voynich project

Built 2026-10-17 18:36 PDT (clock read with `date`). Scope: every metric, percentage, ratio, count or p-value stated in the summary documents listed in §0, checked against the `results_*.txt` / JSON file that holds it. Claims are verified against source files, never against another `.md`. Line numbers are those of the files as they stood at 18:30–18:36 PDT (TEAM-HANDOFF.md, K_RESULTS.md, PLAN.md, PREREG-v2-DRAFT.md and F_RESULTS.md were re-saved at 18:30 and carry N2 banners; the audit's older line numbers are translated where they moved).

Status key: **VERIFIED** = value read in the named source file this session. **STALE** = doc and source disagree, or a later source file supersedes the doc's reading; the row says which is right. **UNSUPPORTED** = no source file holds the number (or the file does not contain it). **UNSUPPORTED-LITERATURE** = a published figure the project cites; source = the research_notes/ or data/external/ text that carries it; "opened" means that text was grepped this session.

Id bands: c-0xx local statistics on the manuscript · c-1xx producer / control results · c-2xx literature · c-3xx data and inventory counts. §G lists the sanctioned rounded prose forms.

## §0 Inputs harvested

docs/AUDIT-2026-10-17.md (§1 rows promoted, §2 contradictions turned into STALE/UNSUPPORTED rows); README.md; TEAM-HANDOFF.md; PLAN.md; PREREG.md; PREREG-v2-DRAFT.md; J_RESULTS.md; K_RESULTS.md; SCOUT-EXTERNAL.md; docs/rounds/{C,E,F,G}_RESULTS.md, FINDINGS.md, INVESTIGATION.md, LEADS-AND-FINDINGS.md, LEADS-CHASE.md, README-a-series.md, RESEARCH-PLAN.md, DOCX_REVIEW.md; reports/Voynich decipherment attempts and failures.md; data/CONTROLS_MANIFEST.md; data/external/README.md.
